document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const salaryCap = 50000;
    const lineupSize = 6;

    }

    // Populate driver settings dynamically
    function populateDriverSettings(drivers) {
        const settingsBody = document.getElementById("driver-settings-body");
        settingsBody.innerHTML = "";

        drivers.forEach((driver, index) => {
            const row = document.createElement("tr");
            row.innerHTML = `
                <td>${driver.name}</td>
                <td>$${driver.salary.toLocaleString()}</td>
                <td>${driver.avgPoints.toFixed(2)}</td>
                <td><input type="checkbox" class="lock-driver" data-index="${index}"></td>
                <td><input type="checkbox" class="exclude-driver" data-index="${index}"></td>
                <td><input type="number" class="min-exposure" data-index="${index}" min="0" max="100" value="0"></td>
                <td><input type="number" class="max-exposure" data-index="${index}" min="0" max="100" value="100"></td>
            `;
            settingsBody.appendChild(row);
        });
    }

    // Generate lineups efficiently
    function generateLineups() {
        const drivers = getDriverData();
        const lineupsOutput = document.getElementById("lineups-output");
        const numLineups = parseInt(document.getElementById("num-lineups").value) || 10;

        lineupsOutput.innerHTML = ""; // Clear previous lineups

        // Filter valid drivers
        const validDrivers = drivers.filter(driver => driver.salary > 0 && driver.avgPoints > 0);
        if (validDrivers.length < lineupSize) {
            lineupsOutput.innerHTML = "<p style='color: red;'>Not enough valid drivers to create a lineup.</p>";
            return;
        }

        // Sort by avgPoints for better scoring
        validDrivers.sort((a, b) => b.avgPoints - a.avgPoints);

        const lineups = [];
        let attempts = 0;

        while (lineups.length < numLineups && attempts < 5000) {
            attempts++;

            const lineup = [];
            let totalSalary = 0;
            let totalPoints = 0;

            for (const driver of validDrivers) {
                if (lineup.length < lineupSize && totalSalary + driver.salary <= salaryCap) {
                    lineup.push(driver);
                    totalSalary += driver.salary;
                    totalPoints += driver.avgPoints;
                }
            }

            if (lineup.length === lineupSize && !lineups.some(l => JSON.stringify(l) === JSON.stringify(lineup))) {
                lineups.push({ lineup, totalSalary, totalPoints });
            }
        }

        // Render top lineups
        lineups
            .sort((a, b) => b.totalPoints - a.totalPoints)
            .slice(0, numLineups)
            .forEach((lineup, index) => {
                const div = document.createElement("div");
                div.innerHTML = `
                    <h3>Lineup ${index + 1}</h3>
                    <ul>
                        ${lineup.lineup.map(d => `<li>${d.name} ($${d.salary}, ${d.avgPoints.toFixed(2)} pts)</li>`).join("")}
                    </ul>
                    <p><strong>Total Salary:</strong> $${lineup.totalSalary}</p>
                    <p><strong>Total Points:</strong> ${lineup.totalPoints.toFixed(2)}</p>
                `;
                lineupsOutput.appendChild(div);
            });
    }

    // Attach event listeners
    document.getElementById("generate-lineups").addEventListener("click", generateLineups);

    // Initial setup
    const drivers = getDriverData();
    populateDriverSettings(drivers);
});

<script data-wp-block-html="js">
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

  const nav = document.querySelector(".wtr-nav");

  if (!nav) return;

  const mobileToggle =
    nav.querySelector(".wtr-mobile-toggle");

  const dropdowns =
    nav.querySelectorAll(".wtr-has-menu");

  const dropdownButtons =
    nav.querySelectorAll(".wtr-top-link");


  /* ---------------------------------------
     Close all dropdowns
     --------------------------------------- */

  function closeAll(except = null) {

    dropdowns.forEach(function (item) {

      if (item === except) return;

      item.classList.remove("wtr-open");

      const button =
        item.querySelector(".wtr-top-link");

      if (button) {
        button.setAttribute(
          "aria-expanded",
          "false"
        );
      }

    });

  }


  /* ---------------------------------------
     Desktop/mobile dropdown clicks
     --------------------------------------- */

  dropdownButtons.forEach(function (button) {

    button.addEventListener(
      "click",
      function (event) {

        event.preventDefault();

        const parent =
          button.closest(".wtr-has-menu");

        const isOpen =
          parent.classList.contains("wtr-open");

        closeAll(parent);

        parent.classList.toggle(
          "wtr-open",
          !isOpen
        );

        button.setAttribute(
          "aria-expanded",
          String(!isOpen)
        );

      }
    );

  });


  /* ---------------------------------------
     Mobile hamburger
     --------------------------------------- */

  if (mobileToggle) {

    mobileToggle.addEventListener(
      "click",
      function () {

        const isOpen =
          nav.classList.contains(
            "wtr-mobile-open"
          );

        nav.classList.toggle(
          "wtr-mobile-open",
          !isOpen
        );

        mobileToggle.setAttribute(
          "aria-expanded",
          String(!isOpen)
        );

        if (isOpen) {
          closeAll();
        }

      }
    );

  }


  /* ---------------------------------------
     Close when clicking outside
     --------------------------------------- */

  document.addEventListener(
    "click",
    function (event) {

      if (!nav.contains(event.target)) {

        closeAll();

        nav.classList.remove(
          "wtr-mobile-open"
        );

        if (mobileToggle) {
          mobileToggle.setAttribute(
            "aria-expanded",
            "false"
          );
        }

      }

    }
  );


  /* ---------------------------------------
     Escape key closes menu
     --------------------------------------- */

  document.addEventListener(
    "keydown",
    function (event) {

      if (event.key === "Escape") {

        closeAll();

        nav.classList.remove(
          "wtr-mobile-open"
        );

        if (mobileToggle) {
          mobileToggle.setAttribute(
            "aria-expanded",
            "false"
          );
        }

      }

    }
  );


  /* ---------------------------------------
     Reset when moving desktop/mobile
     --------------------------------------- */

  window.addEventListener(
    "resize",
    function () {

      if (window.innerWidth > 900) {

        nav.classList.remove(
          "wtr-mobile-open"
        );

        if (mobileToggle) {
          mobileToggle.setAttribute(
            "aria-expanded",
            "false"
          );
        }

      }

    }
  );

});
</script>
